### Frigate

ship_mod_frigate_armor_light = {
	type = ship_mod_slot_armor
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/military_icons/navy_icons/ship_designer_icons/ship_designer_armor_low.dds"
	clicksound = "event:/SFX/UI/ShipDesigner/shipdesigner_modification_armor_light"

	modifier = {
		ship_armor_add = 2
		ship_hit_points_max_add = 70
	}

	construction_goods = { # 1400
		goods_input_hardwood_add = 35
	}

	ai_weight = {
		value = 1
		multiply = ship_mod_market_multiplier

		# SITAI BEGIN information:ship_mod_frigate_armor_light.ai_weight
		# ACTION program_04eaf706407bf9b2 [joint]; signed-envelope consumer action
		if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes } } multiply = {
			value = 1
			# GROUP program_04eaf706407bf9b2_equipment_financing_pressure_negative
			add = { value = 0
				# equipment_ship_mod_frigate_armor_light_default [military]; 国家已实际违约，提高改装融资机会成本；正常借款不惩罚，不代表实际付款
				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes in_default = yes } } add = -0.15 }
			}
			# GROUP program_04eaf706407bf9b2_equipment_material_opportunity_positive
			add = { value = 0
				# equipment_ship_mod_frigate_armor_light_hardwood_opportunity [military]; 候选真实正投入hardwood已观察低价且不短缺，仅给有限材料机会；不是已建造或整体可负担
				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:hardwood mg:hardwood = { market_goods_pricier <= -0.1 market_goods_delta >= 0.0 } } } } add = 0.08 }
			}
			# GROUP program_04eaf706407bf9b2_equipment_material_pressure_hardwood_negative
			add = { value = 0
				# equipment_ship_mod_frigate_armor_light_hardwood_pressure [military]; 候选真实正投入hardwood高价且短缺，仅升档同一材料压力，不与基础短缺重复
				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:hardwood mg:hardwood = { market_goods_delta < 0.0 market_goods_pricier > 0.25 } } } } add = -0.2 }
				# equipment_ship_mod_frigate_armor_light_hardwood_scarcity [military]; 候选真实正投入hardwood已观察市场缺口，增加材料压力；量只作追溯，不估算实际账单
				else_if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:hardwood mg:hardwood = { market_goods_delta < 0.0 } } } } add = -0.1 }
			}
			min = 0.65
			max = 1.08
		} }
		# SITAI END information:ship_mod_frigate_armor_light.ai_weight
	}
}

				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes in_default = yes } } add = -0.15 }
			}
			# GROUP program_217c5607c76c00cb_equipment_material_opportunity_positive

			}
			# GROUP program_217c5607c76c00cb_equipment_material_pressure_copper_negative
			add = { value = 0
				# equipment_ship_mod_frigate_armor_medium_copper_pressure [military]; 候选真实正投入copper高价且短缺，仅升档同一材料压力，不与基础短缺重复

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/military_icons/navy_icons/ship_designer_icons/ship_designer_propulsion_low.dds"
	clicksound = "event:/SFX/UI/ShipDesigner/shipdesigner_modification_propulsion_light"

	modifier = {
		ship_movement_speed_add = 5.5
	}

	construction_goods = { # 16500
		goods_input_engines_add = 65
		goods_input_oil_add = 315
	}

	ai_weight = {
		value = 1
		multiply = ship_mod_market_multiplier
		multiply = ship_mod_jeune_ecole_doctrine
		multiply = ship_mod_coastal_defence_doctrine

		# SITAI BEGIN information:ship_mod_submarine_propulsion_light.ai_weight
		# ACTION program_a78c80433dd11039 [joint]; signed-envelope consumer action
		if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes } } multiply = {
			value = 1
			# GROUP program_a78c80433dd11039_equipment_financing_pressure_negative
			add = { value = 0
				# equipment_ship_mod_submarine_propulsion_light_default [military]; 国家已实际违约，提高改装融资机会成本；正常借款不惩罚，不代表实际付款
				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes in_default = yes } } add = -0.15 }
			}
			# GROUP program_a78c80433dd11039_equipment_material_opportunity_positive
			add = { value = 0
				# equipment_ship_mod_submarine_propulsion_light_oil_opportunity [military]; 候选真实正投入oil已观察低价且不短缺，仅给有限材料机会；不是已建造或整体可负担
				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:oil mg:oil = { market_goods_pricier <= -0.1 market_goods_delta >= 0.0 } } } } add = 0.08 }
				# equipment_ship_mod_submarine_propulsion_light_engines_opportunity [military]; 候选真实正投入engines已观察低价且不短缺，仅给有限材料机会；不是已建造或整体可负担
				else_if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:engines mg:engines = { market_goods_pricier <= -0.1 market_goods_delta >= 0.0 } } } } add = 0.08 }
			}
			# GROUP program_a78c80433dd11039_equipment_material_pressure_engines_negative
			add = { value = 0
				# equipment_ship_mod_submarine_propulsion_light_engines_pressure [military]; 候选真实正投入engines高价且短缺，仅升档同一材料压力，不与基础短缺重复
				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:engines mg:engines = { market_goods_delta < 0.0 market_goods_pricier > 0.25 } } } } add = -0.2 }
				# equipment_ship_mod_submarine_propulsion_light_engines_scarcity [military]; 候选真实正投入engines已观察市场缺口，增加材料压力；量只作追溯，不估算实际账单
				else_if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:engines mg:engines = { market_goods_delta < 0.0 } } } } add = -0.1 }
			}
			# GROUP program_a78c80433dd11039_equipment_material_pressure_oil_negative
			add = { value = 0
				# equipment_ship_mod_submarine_propulsion_light_oil_pressure [military]; 候选真实正投入oil高价且短缺，仅升档同一材料压力，不与基础短缺重复
				if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:oil mg:oil = { market_goods_delta < 0.0 market_goods_pricier > 0.25 } } } } add = -0.2 }
				# equipment_ship_mod_submarine_propulsion_light_oil_scarcity [military]; 候选真实正投入oil已观察市场缺口，增加材料压力；量只作追溯，不估算实际账单
				else_if = { limit = { exists = ROOT ROOT = { is_ai = yes exists = market market = { exists = mg:oil mg:oil = { market_goods_delta < 0.0 } } } } add = -0.1 }
			}
			min = 0.5
			max = 1.08
		} }
		# SITAI END information:ship_mod_submarine_propulsion_light.ai_weight
	}
}

### Destroyer

		ship_armor_add = 15
